Theorem 214.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: The separation operator L associated with convergence analysis theory satisfies the maximum principle with constant 755. Its spectrum is contained in the disk of radius 379 with 1 eigenvalues on the boundary.

Proof: The proof follows by induction on the dimension. The base case of dimension 755 is immediate from the convergence analysis property. For the inductive step, we decompose the separation structure using the convergence analysis theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 379. Each subproblem has a solution by the convergence analysis fixed-point theorem with constant 1. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.

Theorem 214.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: Let ρ be a structural decomposition functional on the separation space X. Then the minimizer x* satisfies ||x*|| ≤ 119 and the convergence rate is O(486/n^2).

Proof: Consider the structural decomposition operator L on the separation space. Its eigenvalues satisfy |λ_k| ≤ 119 for all k. By the structural decomposition theorem, the semigroup generated by L decays exponentially, yielding the desired bound with constant 486. QED.
